Task ID: 107
Agent: orchestrator (Z.ai Code) — solo, non-stop per owner order.

Task: THE PAYOUT RAIL — the withdrawal flow was FAKE MONEY. POST /api/wallet/withdraw debited the wallet the instant a request arrived, wrote a 'processing' transaction nothing anywhere could complete or reject, and left no refund path, while sellerPayouts (the queue /api/supplier/payouts honestly reads) had zero writers — by its own design comment ("creating a payout is a finance/admin action; the previous fake POST that echoed the body back was removed"). Built the full verification circuit under the same doctrine as Task 56: money only leaves through verification.

Work Log:
- SCHEMA: sellerPayouts += destination (the seller's withdrawal target verbatim at request time; reference remains the admin's transfer stamp). db:push + client regen.
- SELLER RAIL (POST+GET /api/wallet/withdraw rewritten): a request creates a PENDING sellerPayouts row and HOLDS the amount — available = wallet balance − the supplier's own pending/processing requests, so the same ৳ can never be double-requested while a payout awaits decision. The wallet is NEVER touched at request time (drill-proven: balance unchanged, zero walletTransactions rows — the exact spot the old flow debited). Supplier-profile gate 403, 'wallet-withdraw' 10/min, 'wallet-withdraw-list' 60/min, Zod STRICT, audits payout_requested.
- ADMIN QUEUE (GET /api/admin/payouts + PATCH /[id]): status filter with 400 on invented values, supplier companyNames, real groupBy stats; COMPLETE debits the wallet + writes the real 'withdrawal' walletTransactions row + stamps reference/processedAt in ONE transaction (complete without a reference → 400 — no untraceable money claims); REJECT frees the hold without moving a taka (nothing was debited, so no refund machinery); decisions are TERMINAL (re-decide → 409); the defensive insufficient-balance guard is proven with a drill-planted past-balance row → 409; audits payout_completed/payout_rejected + payout:changed taps.
- HOLD-AWARE EARNINGS: /api/finance/earnings now serves availablePayout = balance − holds + onHoldForPayouts.
- UI: withdraw-money-page rewritten (three-way balance box Wallet/On hold/Available with the doctrine line, hold-aware client guard, YOUR PAYOUT REQUESTS list with status chips + destination + reference + 'amount held until verified'); admin-payments-page gained the money-rail switcher (Buyer payments ↔ Seller payouts — one console, one doctrine) with self-contained AdminPayoutsManager (stats chips, status tabs, Settle dialog with mandatory reference, Reject dialog, terminal-state copy, verbatim server errors). Also cleaned the Task-106 route files of `_tap`/re-exported handlers (Next route-export validation would refuse them on a production build).
- PLATFORM BUG CAUGHT BY THE DRILL: zod v4 .superRefine() wraps a NON-strict object — unknown keys were silently stripped and a ghost ৳100 payout materialized in run 1. Both payout schemas now .strict(). (Second catch of the same v4 lesson after Task 106 — now recorded as a standing rule: EVERY request schema gets .strict() before it ships.)
- scripts/qa/t107-e2e.mjs: 44/44 PASS on wiped DB ×2 (plus the catching run). Gates ×7; validation ×5; hold math edge-to-edge (400 → 600 available → double-request 400 → exactly-at-edge 201 → 1000 held / 0 available); no-debit-at-request proof via raw DB; complete tx proof via raw walletTransactions (−600, balanceAfter 400, reference in description); terminal 409s both directions; hold freed on reject with wallet untouched; planted-row guard 409; hold-aware earnings {400 − 150 = 250}; stats + audit counts exact (3/1/2).
- BROWSER QA (gateway :81, real UI login): seller files a REAL ৳400 bKash request → toast states the doctrine verbatim → request list shows PENDING VERIFICATION + destination → balance box flips ৳600 available / ৳400 held → admin Seller payouts rail shows the queue row with destination → Settle with reference BKASH-TRX-107QA → toast 'wallet debited' → completed tab shows the stamp → raw DB {wallet 600, withdrawal −400 ledger row with reference in description, payout completed + processedAt, audits exact} → mobile 375×812 zero overflow on both surfaces → console clean.

Task ID: 111
Agent: orchestrator (Z.ai Code) — solo, non-stop per owner order.

Task: THE REWARD TASKS RAIL — the last read-without-writer table from the audit queue (couponEarningTasks, read by GET /api/coupons/earn since day one) AND the round it was deferred for: the POST was a MONEY PRINTER. Any authenticated user POSTing any taskId minted a ৳ voucher instantly — no proof of task completion, no one-claim guard, no rate limit, no audit, and a self-reported random code. A task is money; completion is now PROVEN from the buyer's real rows.

Work Log:
- SCHEMA: couponEarningTasks += targetCount Int @default(1) (additive; table empty per doctrine). db:push + client regen. (Edit-tool mishap introduced a stray duplicate `model couponEarningTasks {` heading mid-edit — caught by the tool's own diff echo, removed, db:push proved integrity.)
- SRC/LIB/REWARD-TASKS.TS (shared contracts): the console may only mint tasks the server can PROVE — REWARD_TASK_TYPES is the seven provable types (first_order | review | wishlist | bulk | milestone | spin | checkin); share / referral / review_photo are REFUSED AT CREATE with the honest reason (no proof source exists in the schema: no share tracking, no referral model, supplierReviews has no images) — a reward on them would be a money printer. countProof() counts each buyer's REAL rows: orders via subOrders.some(status notIn cancelled/returned) (orders has NO status column — status lives on subOrders; the drill's tsc + first run caught my wrong predicate), supplierReviews, wishlists, spinWinResults (prizeType != try-again), dailyCheckins. BULK SEMANTICS: targetCount is the per-line QUANTITY THRESHOLD — proof = the buyer's largest single order line via orderItems._max(quantity) through non-cancelled subOrders (run-1 bug: I compared "number of qualifying lines" against the threshold and a real qty-3 line was refused — caught by the drill, fixed to max-line semantics).
- ADMIN RAILS (GET/POST /api/admin/reward-tasks + PATCH/DELETE /[id]): requireUserType 401-first, 'admin-reward-tasks-list' 60/min + 'admin-reward-tasks-write' 20/min, Zod STRICT create (rewardBDT 0..10000 cap, iconColor hex, targetCount 1..1000, sortOrder), list carries per-task claimCount (real userCoupons groupBy) + stats {total, active, parked, totalClaims}; PATCH touches only arrived keys with REAL before/after audit; isActive=false parks (day-one public reader already filters it); DELETE stamps liveClaimsAtDeletion — vouchers already earned keep existing (userCoupons rows are standalone), the removal is on the record.
- PLATFORM BUG CAUGHT BY THE DRILL (third zod-v4 catch): rewardTaskPatchSchema = createSchema.partial() LEAKED THE CREATE-TIME DEFAULTS — an empty PATCH silently overwrote a real task (targetCount 2→1, sortOrder 1→0) because .partial() on fields declared .optional().default(x) re-applies the defaults. Fixed by writing the patch schema EXPLICITLY with no defaults; empty PATCH → 400 'No fields to update'. Standing rule re-confirmed: patch schemas are hand-built, never derived from defaulted create schemas.
- BUYER RAIL (/api/coupons/earn rewritten): GET is public+optional-auth — signed-in callers get claimed flags AND honest per-task progress {count, required, met} from countProof (guests get plain tasks, no fields); POST is the verifier: 401-first → 'coupon-earn-claim' 10/min → strict body (unknown keys refused) → active task or 404 → one-claim-per-buyer-forever 409 (dup re-checked inside the create tx) → countProof short of target → 403 with the honest shortfall ("needs 1 — you have 0", "needs a single order line with quantity ≥ 5 — you have none yet") + progress numbers → voucher created in one tx (EARN-<TYPE>-<8hex> via crypto.randomBytes, 14-day validity, savingsBDT/minOrder at the house convention) → auditLogs reward_claimed (voucher + task + proofCount) → real notification → tapAllAdmins reward:claimed.
- UI: admin-content seventh tab 'Rewards' (self-contained AdminRewardTasksManager — stats chips, live/park switches, claimCount chips, create/edit dialog with the per-type rule text under the selector, type LOCKED while editing, AlertDialog deletes with the "vouchers stay in buyer vaults" copy, verbatim server errors). Buyer earn-coupons-page rewritten: no alert() (house toasts), claimed rows flip to CLAIMED chip + 'In vault' button, Progress bars for multi-target tasks, sign-in-gated claim, verbatim server refusals, honest empty state, type icons for the real seven-type vocabulary. NOTE: the pageId 'earn-coupons' resolves REAL_PAGES → chunk-misc → the standalone page file; the older embedded EarnCouponsPage inside generic-info-page.tsx is a DEAD duplicate its specialType branch can no longer reach (left in place, flagged here so nobody "fixes" the wrong component).

Public honest-zero; admin gates ×4; invented types refused ×3 (the share/referral/review_photo refusal message names the proof-source reason); strict/validation ×7; create + public reflection (sortOrder-ordered, claimCount 0, claimed/progress fields only for authed callers); buyer-with-zero-rows 403 carrying real progress numbers; claim strict/missing/phantom; PROOF-DRIVEN claims ×4 (first_order counts BOTH orders, milestone 2-of-2, bulk max-line 3 ≥ 2, wishlist 0→1 after the real wishlist POST, checkin after the real check-in POST); re-claim 409; voucher verbatim (code prefix, title, label, savings, min-order, ~14-day validity); all-claimed GET; vault ≥ 5; notifications ≥ 5; park vanishing + console retention; edit-only-arrived-keys; empty PATCH 400; phantom PATCH/DELETE 404; delete + re-delete 404; final stats {4 rows, claims 4}; audits exact {created 5, updated 2, deleted 1, claimed 5} with before/after + liveClaimsAtDeletion + proofCount metadata; exactly 5 voucher rows (no farmer duplicates). Task ID: 117-b
Agent: orchestrator (Z.ai Code) — solo, non-stop per owner order.

Task: THE OOM FORENSICS + the dev-server memory cap — root-causing this sandbox lifetime's "silent reaper" and fixing what the repo can fix.

Work Log:
- FORENSICS: dmesg re-count found THREE kernel OOM kills (not one — the earlier single-kill reading predates the later deaths): next-server killed at ~2.5-2.6GB anon-rss, kernel times 501s / 4902s / 4983s. The 4GB sandbox + headless Chrome (~0.5GB) + Turbopack's in-memory module graphs (every server restart re-compiles every visited route — .next does NOT carry the dev module cache) = the death spiral: restart → cold compiles → ~2.6GB → OOM kill → repeat. Most "silent whole-tree deaths" were these kills (the surviving keep-alive loop then restarted into the same spiral).
- THE REPO FIX: next.config.ts experimental.turbopackMemoryLimit. First attempt 1536 was TOO TIGHT — Turbopack aborted mid-compile trying to hold it (server died compiling "/" with no OOM, no trace; reproducible). 2560 is the working value: ten of the heaviest page chunks (home, orders, cart, checkout, store-page, seller-order-management, admin-content, order-detail, seller-promotions, mini-games) all compiled and served 200 in ONE server lifetime with next-server RSS measured at 1665 MB — versus the uncapped 2.5-2.6GB that the kernel killed. The OOM class is closed for single-server windows.
- RESIDUAL (honest, open): a silent no-trace death class remains — the server died once right after the 10-route curl window (no OOM #4, no panic, no crash line) when the browser session opened. Cause not yet isolated (suspect: client-bundle compile bursts on top of the cap, or a Turbopack edge aborting without output). Future rounds: if a no-trace death follows a compile-heavy window, restart and continue — the cap keeps RSS recoverable (~1.6GB) instead of the 2.6GB OOM spiral.
- ENVIRONMENT LESSON (proven): Turbopack's dev module graph lives in MEMORY ONLY — restarting the dev server does NOT warm-start from .next; every visited route recompiles from scratch per server lifetime. Keep server lifetimes aligned to QA batches.
- My own footgun, recorded: malformed request URLs ("//?page=...") throw ERR_INVALID_URL inside the request handler (TypeError × N in dev.log) — one burst coincided with a crash window. Never build test URLs with a leading slash on top of a base that already has one.
- tsc 0 whole project; eslint 0.

Stage Summary:
- The "silent reaper" is now a solved, documented, PARTLY-fixed environment: the OOM half is fixed in-repo (memory cap, 1665MB proven), the residual no-trace half is documented with the restart-and-continue protocol.
- Pushed with the owner-provided PAT (never stored in tracked files).

Work Log:
- CRON MANDATE honored FIRST: Job 467469 deleted (cron list → delete → success). NO CRON order now holds with zero jobs.

Neither my-wallet nor admin pages knew topups existed at all.
- SCHEMA (additive): walletTopups += claimDetails Json? (the payments.gatewayResponse twin); docs: status += 'rejected', provider += nagad/rocket/upay/bank_transfer. db:push proven by PRAGMA (claimDetails PRESENT).
- CLAIM ROUTE rewritten (POST /api/wallet/topup): 401-first → 'wallet-topup-claim' 10/min → Zod STRICT {amount 10..100000, method enum, transactionReference REQUIRED /^[A-Za-z0-9\-_]{6,64}$/, senderNumber?, note? ≤300} → reference uniqueness (findUnique + P2002 race → 409, distinguishable you/other messages) → ONE create {status:'pending', claimDetails{method,senderNumber?,note?,submittedAt}} + auditLogs 'wallet_topup_claimed' (metadata states the no-money note) → broadcast wallet:topup-submitted to every admin room. NO wallets row, NO balance change, NO ledger row. GET stays own-rows (take 50).

- ADMIN DECISION (POST /api/admin/topups/[id]/verify) — the ONLY route that credits a manual claim: 401-first → 'admin-topup-verify' 20/min → 404 no-leak → Zod {action verify|reject, note?} → pending guard 409 (`Top-up already <status>`) → ONE tx: verify = wallet find-or-create INSIDE the tx + balance increment + walletTransactions deposit row with REAL balanceAfter + topup{credited, creditedAt, gatewayTrxId=reference}; reject = topup{rejected} ONLY → auditLogs wallet_topup_verified/rejected with before/after {topupStatus, walletBalance} + actorType admin → broadcast to admin rooms → REAL notifications row for the claimant either way. claimDetails gains the verification annex (payments policy).

Task ID: 124
Agent: orchestrator (Z.ai Code) — solo, non-stop per owner order.

Task: THE LAST TWO ORPHANS — /api/auth/refresh and /api/profile/completion-status were the only remaining endpoints with zero UI consumers. refresh turned out to be UNWIREABLE as written (rotation returned a JSON token but never set the httpOnly auth-token cookie — no client could ever complete the rotation); completion-status had a perfect response with no reader. Both are now live, drilled, and browser-proven — including a load-time race the first design had, caught and designed out.

Work Log:
- refresh route FIXED: now calls setAuthCookie(response, newToken) — the rotated httpOnly cookie lands in the browser jar, making single-use rotation actually completable (old session destroyed → fresh 7-day Set-Cookie → the browser's next request carries the new session).
- Shell wiring with a designed-out race: first cut rotated on EVERY page load — which destroys the token the page's Bearer fetches are about to use (extractToken gives Bearer PRECEDENCE over the cookie, so a stale store token 401s every Bearer call). Caught during browser QA (buyer-profile rendered without its completion card). FINAL DESIGN: NEW GET /api/auth/session-info (non-destructive probe: authenticateRequest + auth.sessionExpiresAt) → the shell (use-session-refresh hook) probes once per page load and only POSTs refresh when < 2 days of session remain; normal loads never race their own auth. validateSession now returns sessionExpiresAt (additive AuthResult field).
- Hook syncs the store: on rotation the response token is written via useAuthStore.getState().setToken (Bearer stays in lockstep with the cookie); a zylod:session-refreshed event is dispatched for future listeners.
- completion-status CONSUMED: buyer-profile-page dashboard tab gained the "Profile strength" card (data-testid profile-completion-card / completion-pct) — overall % bar + per-section chips (✓/○) from the REAL sections; fetch rides the page's existing token pattern and re-runs with the other profile fetches.
- DRILL scripts/qa/t124-e2e.mjs — **25/0 on a fresh wipe**: gates (no-token 400, REALLY-presented garbage token 401 — first draft's "garbage" test sent NO cookie and correctly got 400, drill fixed); session-info (anon 401, authed 200 with ~7-day expiresInMs); rotation semantics (200, identity echo, token length, 7-day window, session count STABLE across rotation (destroy+create), the ROTATED cookie authenticates /api/profile/me, second rotation chain holds); device independence (a second login of the same buyer keeps working across device 1's rotations); completion math (delivery_address 0 before → 100 after a REAL address create; overall = exact mean 67===67; supplier branch populated). Drill-side fix: second device must LOGIN not register (409 otherwise).
- REGRESSION-SAFE: no existing drill touches refresh/session-info/completion-status; tsc 0, eslint 0.
- BROWSER QA (real UI logins, gateway :81): buyer-profile completion card renders the EXACT drill numbers (67% + ✓ Personal Information / ○ Contact Verification / ✓ Delivery Address — honest per-row truth); END-TO-END ROTATION PROVEN LIVE: the buyer's session row aged to +1 day (SQL), one page reload → the shell probed → rotated → DB shows the old row destroyed and a FRESH 7-day session created → the browser stayed fully authenticated (completion-status 200 via the new cookie, card still rendering, store synced). Console clean, dev.log clean.
- HONEST NOTE: buyer-profile at 375px shows a pre-existing 3px horizontal overflow (378 vs 375) from an edge SVG icon — present with AND without the completion card; not introduced by this task, recorded for a future styling pass.
- tsc 0 whole project; eslint 0; DB wiped 139×0 before commit.

Stage Summary:
- Zero orphaned endpoints remain from the cohesion audit: every /api route now either has a real consumer or is an externally-called surface (webhooks, native bridge, driver app).
- Sessions now keep active users alive honestly: near-expiry rotation with single-use semantics, httpOnly cookie completion, store sync, device independence — and the load-time race is designed out, not patched over.
- REMAINING FAKE/INCOMPLETE: none found this round. Known cosmetic: buyer-profile 3px svg overflow at 375px (pre-existing).

Task ID: 130
Agent: orchestrator (Z.ai Code) — solo, non-stop per owner order.

Task: THE "Up to 0% off" LIE — the candidate Task 129 recorded. The desktop PDP's Bulk Discounts Trust tile claimed "Up to {savingsPercent}% off", but savingsPercent measures the discount at the CURRENT quantity — always 0% at MOQ — so a product whose tiers genuinely give 10% at 200+ pcs displayed "Up to 0% off".

Work Log:
- ROOT CAUSE: line 541's savingsPercent = (basePrice − applicablePrice)/basePrice where applicablePrice follows the CURRENT quantity (canonical wholesale rule). Correct for the inline "Save X%" badge (line 999, which properly hides at 0) — wrong semantics for a tile promising the product's maximum.
- FIX: new maxSavingsPercent useMemo sweeps the WHOLE priceTiers ladder vs basePrice (clamped ≥ 0) — the real maximum a bulk buyer can reach. Tile now shows "Up to {maxSavingsPercent}% off", and when the product genuinely has no discounts it says the honest "No tiered discounts set" instead of a nonsensical "Up to 0% off". The inline badge keeps current-quantity semantics.
- VERIFIED: tsc 0, eslint 0. Seed re-run on a fresh wipe (both products approved via real APIs). Agent: orchestrator (Z.ai Code) — solo, non-stop per owner order.

Task: THE COUPON CIRCUIT — the orphan scan's biggest true orphan. /api/coupons/validate (a complete validation engine: active/window/usage-limit/min-order/discount math) had ZERO consumers: the checkout input attached codes client-side with discount 0, so buyers got NO live feedback and NO discount preview — bad codes only surfaced after the order refused (Task 74), and the buyer never saw what a coupon was worth before paying.

Work Log:
- ORPHAN VERDICTS (fresh scan, 14 candidates → 4 new): /api/coupons/validate TRUE (this task); /api/invoices/[id] + /api/orders/[id]/invoice TRUE but deferred (the order-invoice page builds from GET /api/orders/[id] — wiring them is a document task, next rounds); /api/supplier/queries/[id] PARTIAL (list consumed by customer-queries-page, the [id] GET/PATCH not — next rounds); the other 10 were Task 128's already-settled FALSE/EXTERNAL verdicts.
- REAL ROUTE BUG (found by reading the money path before wiring): validate ALWAYS computed percentage math and ignored discountType — the schema has percentage|fixed where 'fixed' stores the flat ৳ in discountPercent, and POST /api/orders pays `min(discountPercent, totalAmount)` for fixed. A fixed coupon previewed the wrong ৳ (or ৳0). Fixed: the formula now mirrors the order write EXACTLY (fixed → min(flat, orderAmount); percentage → orderAmount × pct capped by maxDiscount ?? ∞) and the response carries discountType.
- CHECKOUT WIRING: Apply → POST /api/coupons/validate { code, orderAmount: subtotal } (goods-only base matches the server's coupon base — shipping and promotions enter later server-side); failure shows the route's REAL reason verbatim; success sets { code, discount, discountType }, renders the emerald chip "Validated — ৳X off this cart…", a summary discount line, and the payableTotal. STALE-PROOF: a ref-tracked subtotal re-validates whenever the goods subtotal moves (qty changes) — a coupon that stops being valid clears itself with the endpoint's real reason. Enter submits; Apply shows "Checking…" while in flight.
- ONE-NUMBER RULE: new payableTotal = max(0, orderTotal − discount) — the Order Summary total, the "Pay ৳X from wallet balance" label, and the insufficient-balance copy ALL quote the same figure (browser caught the wallet label showing ৳1,000 beside a ৳900 summary — fixed before commit).
- DRILL scripts/qa/t131-e2e.mjs — **31/0 on a fresh wipe**: 6 coupon fixtures (percentage+cap, fixed, min-order, usage-limit-1, future window, expired — the expired one DB-written since the create route refuses already-dead windows); validate gates (no code 400, unknown 404 valid:false, expired/not-yet-valid/min-order 400 with real reasons); math parity (10% of 1000 = 100; 10% of 5000 → capped 200; fixed 100; fixed vs ৳50 order → 50); THE REAL ORDER PATH (wallet-paid orders: T131WELCOME → total ৳900 + usedCount 1; T131FLAT → ৳900; T131ONCE first ok then 409 usage limit; T131MIN2K → 409 names ৳2,000 minimum; T131DEAD/NOSUCH → 409 with real reasons).
- REGRESSION: t74 (the coupon order-write drill) 28/0 on a fresh wipe.
- BROWSER QA (real UI login, gateway :81): desktop 1280×800 — apply T131WELCOME → chip + "− ৳100" line + Goods total ৳900 + "Pay ৳900 from wallet balance" (one number everywhere); apply T131DEAD → red "Coupon has expired" alert, total unchanged; mobile 375×812 — same states, scrollWidth exactly 375. Fixed: discount = subtotal + shipping − totalAmount (real money only — campaign/coupon effects already live in the priced lines); VAT renders as a labeled "regulatory reference" that is never summed into the renamed "Total (as charged)". Money identity drilled: subtotal + shipping − discount === grandTotal.

Task: GROUP-BUY POOLS THAT NEVER PAID OFF — the pool page promised "reserved N units at the volume rate of ৳X" but nothing honored it: anyone could create a campaign at any price for any product, joins raced, completion did nothing, and no order path consumed the pool rate. The wholesale-pooling pillar was a promise without teeth. Built the teeth.

Work Log:
- CREATION is a seller pricing commitment: only the product's OWN supplier opens a pool (ownership 403; buyers 403 SUPPLIER_PROFILE_REQUIRED), Zod strict, the rate must be a real discount (above factory rate → 422 "that would be a fake discount"), target ≥ MOQ, 5/min, auditLogs 'group_buy_created'. SELLER UI: a Pool action + dialog on every approved listing row (target/rate/duration).
- JOINS are atomic and honest: participant + counter in ONE transaction (compound-unique per buyer; re-joining tops up the SAME row); over-target joins refused 409 with the REAL remaining units; a filled pool answers 409 "already filled — N of M" (not the lying 404); completion fans out real notifications to every participant + the supplier.
- THE TEETH: the order engine prices participants' lines at the pool rate up to their reservation — plain lines only, blended unitPrice on straddling lines (line identity unitPrice × qty === totalPrice preserved), redemption increments in the SAME transaction (never double-spent, never survives a failed order), only ever a discount, receipt surfaces groupPoolDiscount.
- Schema: groupBuyParticipants.redeemedQty + @@unique([campaignId, userId]) (db:push).
- DRILL scripts/qa/t141-e2e.mjs — 41/0 on a fresh wipe: creation gates ×6, audit row, join gates, same-row top-up, fill, filled-409, fan-out rows, THE MONEY (40-unit order at ৳800 + receipt ৳8,000; straddle blended ৳68,000/৳850 + receipt ৳12,000; capped at 100; consumed pool grants nothing; non-participant pays rule price).
- REGRESSIONS: t115 92/0 (the order monolith), t74 28/0 (coupon order-write), t131 31/0 (coupon validate).
- BROWSER (real logins, gateway :81): seller opens the pool through the NEW listings dialog (real row + toast), buyer joins through the REAL detail page → "Total Reserved: 20 / 20 — 100% Target Achieved" + "Pool filled … locked at ৳800 each" notification; the join form is replaced by the honest filled-state on completed pools; mobile 375 exact. Buyer dashboard totalSavingsBDT now includes used bound vouchers (face-value convention matching the existing stat).
- UI TRUTH (found in the browser pass): the redeem page's min-order copy claimed ৳500/1,000/2,500/5,000 where the API/checkout honor ৳150/300/750/1,500 (3× rule) — every number was a lie; the ৳1,000 tier was secretly a wallet credit behind a minOrder-0 sentinel — now honestly labeled '৳1,000 Wallet Credit'; NEW custom-redemption card exposes what the API always honored (any whole amount ≥ 100 points → ৳ value, min order 3×) — a 200-point buyer was previously locked out of redeeming entirely.

- DEFECT #3 (fake success + half-persisted) — app-network-settings-page called /api/app/settings, ignored the response, and showed "Network rules updated!" on EVERY outcome (401, 500, network error all read as success); only 1 of 3 fields (timeout) was sent; dataSaver + dnsOverHttps were decorative. Rebuilt: Storage Manager tile shows REAL localStorage bytes (useLocalStorageUsageBytes — counts every key+value char × 2), Cache tile shows prefs.imageCacheMaxMb, Check Updates reads the real public app_version (honest "v—" when no row exists), Offline Package reflects prefs.autoSyncIntervalMins. Reads /api/profile for the userType (session-info doesn't return it).
